### codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md
  75: The shared path includes `SharedMarketSource`, `SharedMarketConsumer`,
  76: `SharedAnalysisDispatcher`, cutover/readiness logic, and local historical
  77: tables such as `shared_market_event_delivery`, `shared_market_consumer_state`,
  78: and `shared_market_health`. These records remain evidence and must not be
  79: dropped, reset, or used to manufacture an approval.
  80: 
  81: The collector event/cursor source is `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event`
  82: and `market_data_stream_cursor`. In the target local-candle runtime, the
  83: trader must not connect to, read, or write these collector tables.
  84: 
  85: `V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes` is an existing Java Flyway migration
  86: that requires the collector event table for a fresh installation. A trader-only
  87: bootstrap policy is therefore **pending** and remains a release blocker. Do
  88: not edit applied Flyway migrations or Flyway history.
  89: 
  90: ## Implemented, unverified: local-candle architecture (FIX-153)
  91: 
  92: The implemented intended mode is `shared-market.mode=OFF` with trader-owned Binance kline
  93: ingestion and local candle writes. The shared executors and direct-ingestion
  94: path must never run together.
  95: 
  96: Implemented safeguards:
  97: 
  98: - Candle writes are monotonic: a late open update cannot reopen or overwrite a
  99:   closed candle.
 100: - A closed-candle transition is admitted exactly once using an atomic upsert
 101:   result plus in-process admission coordination.
 102: - A bounded local detector recovers a closed candle that was committed before
 103:   the in-memory event/dispatcher could start analysis.
 104: - Fresh candles may execute; stale candles are analysed historically only, or
 105:   their already-owned work is expired without business execution.
 106: - Analysis lanes are bounded. Under overload, 1m may retain only the latest
 107:   pending candle; higher intervals use an explicit bounded FIFO policy.
 108: - Historic shared `REVIEW_REQUIRED` evidence must not block OFF-mode work.
 109: - OFF-mode health/admin endpoints must use local-candle health and must not
 110:   query retired shared-market tables.
 111: - Current REST backfill can finalise an interrupted open row but cannot mint a
 112:   runtime close token or dispatch analysis. Runtime close tokens plus
 113:   provenance keep backfill out of detector admission. The approved, pending
 114:   automatic-reconciliation extension will also insert an exact wholly absent
 115:   candle only inside the active quality window, with `BACKFILL` provenance and
 116:   the same no-token/no-dispatch boundary.
 117: - Observed local behavior (2026-10-09): a WebSocket reconnect at a candle-close
 118:   boundary can leave the already-persisted 1m/5m rows open because Binance does
 119:   not replay the final closed-kline event. The current detector recovers missing
 120:   analysis admission, not missing close facts. An authoritative, bounded REST
 121:   repair may therefore finalise the exact `closed=0` row or, under the approved
 122:   gap-backfill scope, insert an exact absent identity. It must preserve existing
 123:   provenance, use `BACKFILL` for an insertion, create no close-transition token,
 124:   and never dispatch historical work. Automatic reconciliation of this reconnect
 125:   case remains a FIX-153 release gate; the one-time local repair is evidence,
 126:   not proof that the gap is closed.
 127: - Approved pending reconciliation design: gap backfill is insert-only (no
 128:   duplicate-key update); it can never alter a live row that appears during a
 129:   REST request. Candidate selection is bounded to the active quality window;
 130:   terminal audit records are not retried. Every candle/work route must produce
 131:   a durable terminal outcome or remain explicitly retryable after a transient
 132:   failure. Historical-only processing has no execution authority.
 133: - The pending universal mutation barrier is an in-memory drain participant,
 134:   not a database or persistent trading fence. Outside drain it must not delay,
 135:   queue, or block fresh trading, signals, or protection. Open-position
 136:   protection during application replacement remains an unresolved release gate.
 137: - Local source now contains the first reconciliation implementation slice:
 138:   V107 durable audit/outcome tables, update-only authoritative open-row repair,
 139:   pure insert-only gap backfill, a dedicated recovery executor, a reconnect
 140:   request hook, and drain participation. It is disabled by default and remains
 141:   unvalidated on MySQL; it has no deployment approval.
 142: - Direct prices are normalised to `DECIMAL(30,12)` before persistence,
 143:   protection and completion. Equal timestamps have one canonical receipt.
 144: - Every processing attempt is freshness-checked after normal locks using the
 145:   database-to-exchange correction. JVM-origin timestamp decisions use the
 146:   separately measured JVM-to-exchange correction. The measurement performs no
 147:   HTTP under execution locks and uses a bounded five-second time-only client.
 148:   General Binance REST calls use the configured request timeout, and scheduled
 149:   clock sampling has a four-thread Spring scheduler pool.
 150:   Bootstrap obtains time/klines before opening its local candle-write
 151:   transaction, so a stalled REST request does not retain database resources.
 152:   REST bootstrap gets Binance server time before klines.
 153: - An ADMIN-only drain stops analysis admission, exits the JVM on successful
 154:   quiesce, and resumes admission on timeout. Price protection pauses during
 155:   the controlled drain window.
### claude-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md
  30: | Schema | Owner | Trader use |
  31: |---|---|---|
  32: | `crypto_ai` | Trader | Only runtime DB in OFF mode. |
  33: | `crypto_ai_v2` | Collector | Read in LIVE/OBSERVE via `SharedMarketSource` read-only pool. Must not be touched in OFF mode. |
  34: 
  35: `V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes` (Java migration) adds indexes to
  36: `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event`; a fresh trader-only install fails
  37: without that schema. Release blocker; do not edit applied migrations.
  38: 
  39: ## Ingestion modes (`shared-market.mode`, default `OFF`)
  40: 
  41: - **OFF (FIX-153)** ƒ?" trader connects to Binance and writes `crypto_ai.candle`.
  42:   `LocalTraderInstance` requires an absolute `local-candle.instance-lock-path`
  43:   (env `LOCAL_TRADER_INSTANCE_LOCK`) and holds an OS file lock: one trader
  44:   process per host. Empty value fails OFF-mode startup.
  45: - **LIVE / OBSERVE (legacy/shared)** ƒ?" `SharedMarketConsumer` polls collector
  46:   stream tables into local `shared_market_event_delivery`;
  47:   `SharedAnalysisDispatcher` drives analysis. Direct ingestion entry points
  48:   (`BinanceWebSocketManager`, `BinanceKlineService.processKline`,
  49:   `MarketDataService.importCandles`, `MarketDataBootstrapService`) are
  50:   disabled in LIVE. The two paths must never run together.
  51: 
  52: ## Primary flow (OFF mode)
  53: 
  54: ```text
  55: Binance WS (combined stream, enabled coins x 1m/5m/1h)
  56:  -> BinanceWebSocketManager (owner thread "fix131-ws-owner", generation gate)
  57:  -> BinanceWebSocketHandler.handleTextMessage (socket thread, synchronous)
  58:  -> BinanceKlineService.processKline        (@Transactional NEVER, drain check)
  59:  -> KlineTransactionCoordinator.process     (each phase REQUIRES_NEW, READ_COMMITTED, 30s)
  60:      1 INPUT        CandleRepository.upsertRuntime + MarketPriceEventService.recordPending (1m)
  61:      2 PROTECTION   LivePositionProtectionService.onPrice (1m; one retry on initial-lock deadlock)
  62:      3 OBSERVER     PriceMoveMonitorService.onPrice (errors swallowed)
  63:      4 CLOSE        publish CandleClosedEvent only if this receipt won the 0->1 close token
  64:  -> CandleClosedEventListener (AFTER_COMMIT, not @Async)
  65:  -> CandleAnalysisDispatcher (per symbol|interval lane)
  66:  -> candleAnalysisExecutor (core 8 / max 16 / queue 200, AbortPolicy)
  67:  -> CandleClosedAnalysisWorker.processShared
  68:  -> TechnicalIndicatorService -> AnalysisService -> TradeSignal + signal_processing_work
  69:  -> SignalProcessingCoordinator -> PaperTradingService -> WalletAutoExecutionService
  70: ```
  71: 
  72: All candle writes and live position protection run on the single WebSocket
  73: socket thread, one message at a time. Analysis never runs on it.
  74: 
  75: ## Candle persistence
  76: 
  77: - `CandleRepository.upsertRuntime/write`: `INSERT ... ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE`
  78:   on `uk_candle_symbol_interval_open_time`. Once `closed=1` every column keeps
  79:   its old value (monotonic). RUNTIME writes set `close_transition_token` only
  80:   on the 0->1 transition; the read-back token equal to this receipt's UUID is
  81:   the sole authority to dispatch a close. BACKFILL writes never set a token.
  82: - `domain/Candle` is now a read DTO.
  83: 
  84: ## Recovery and gaps
  85: 
  86: - `LocalClosedCandleDetector` (every 2s, OFF only): re-admits tokened, non-
  87:   BACKFILL closed candles with no `trade_signal` and no
  88:   `signal_processing_work` (up to 6 retries). Repairs commit-to-dispatch gaps,
  89:   **not** missing close facts.
  90: - `CandleGapDiagnostics` (every 10s, 72h horizon): logs `DB_CANDLE_GAP` only,
  91:   `repair=false`.
  92: - REST backfill (`MarketDataService.importCandles`, BACKFILL provenance,
  93:   skips still-open candle, server time fetched before klines, no HTTP inside
  94:   the transaction) runs only at startup (`MarketDataBootstrapService`) or coin
  95:   activation.
  96: - **Gap:** a WebSocket reconnect at a candle-close boundary can leave rows at
  97:   `closed=0` (Binance does not replay the final closed kline). Nothing repairs
  98:   this automatically; with `maximum-missing-candles: 0` it blocks analysis for
  99:   that symbol/interval. Deployment gate.
 100: 
 101: ## WebSocket lifecycle
 102: 
 103: `BinanceWebSocketManager` ticks every 500 ms on its owner thread; connect and
 104: close run on separate workers. Each attempt gets a new
 105: `BinanceWebSocketHandler` + `GenerationGate` (PENDING -> ADMITTING -> REVOKED);

codex
## FINDINGS

The working tree contains a substantial but unverified implementation of the pending local-candle direction. In `shared-market.mode=OFF`, the trader—not the collector—owns Binance WebSocket ingestion, REST bootstrap, local candle writes, close admission, and asynchronous analysis dispatch.

`LIVE` retains the collector-dependent architecture. `OBSERVE` is architecturally unsafe: it enables both the trader WebSocket path and shared-market consumer path concurrently.

Automatic authoritative reconciliation exists in source but is disabled by checked-in configuration. Consequently, the required automatic handling of a missed Binance close event is not active by default.

A trader-only Flyway baseline, `B105`, exists and is intended to bypass historical migrations including `V103` for a fresh install. This contradicts AGENTS.md and both architecture documents’ categorical statement that fresh trader-only bootstrap is blocked by `V103`. Source-only review cannot establish whether the baseline works with the configured Flyway/MySQL combination.

## EVIDENCE

- **WebSocket ownership in OFF:** `BinanceWebSocketManager` starts unless mode is `LIVE`, then the handler invokes trader-owned `BinanceKlineService.processKline`.
  - [BinanceWebSocketManager.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/client/binance/websocket/BinanceWebSocketManager.java:28)
  - [BinanceWebSocketManager.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/client/binance/websocket/BinanceWebSocketManager.java:95)
  - [BinanceKlineService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BinanceKlineService.java:55)

- **REST/bootstrap ownership:** `MarketDataBootstrapService` invokes local REST import outside `LIVE`; OFF bootstrap is explicitly data-only.
  - [MarketDataBootstrapService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/MarketDataBootstrapService.java:49)
  - [MarketDataBootstrapService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/MarketDataBootstrapService.java:94)
  - [MarketDataBootstrapService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/MarketDataBootstrapService.java:105)
  - [MarketDataService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/MarketDataService.java:31)

- **Persistence:** OFF writes local `candle`; LIVE rejects local candle writes and reads via the collector source. Runtime writes are monotonic and only the token-owning `0→1` close transition admits analysis.
  - [SharedMarketSource.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketSource.java:20)
  - [SharedMarketSource.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketSource.java:45)
  - [CandleRepository.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/repository/CandleRepository.java:99)
  - [CandleRepository.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/repository/CandleRepository.java:109)
  - [CandleRepository.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/repository/CandleRepository.java:169)

- **Close ordering and dispatch:** input, protection, observer, then close-event publication occur as distinct transactions. The event listener is after-commit; the dispatcher uses an executor rather than the WebSocket thread.
  - [KlineTransactionCoordinator.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/infrastructure/transaction/KlineTransactionCoordinator.java:36)
  - [BinanceKlineService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BinanceKlineService.java:101)
  - [CandleClosedEventListener.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/indicator/event/CandleClosedEventListener.java:30)
  - [CandleAnalysisDispatcher.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/indicator/event/CandleAnalysisDispatcher.java:106)

- **Recovery:** update-only authoritative recovery and insert-if-absent gap backfill exist, record terminal audit/outcome state, and never generate a close token. But they run only when explicitly enabled and mode is OFF.
  - [LocalCandleReconciliationService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/local/LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:44)
  - [LocalCandleReconciliationService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/local/LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:92)
  - [CandleRepository.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/repository/CandleRepository.java:132)
  - [CandleRepository.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/repository/CandleRepository.java:152)
  - [application.yml](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/application.yml:40)

- **Collector coupling:** OFF does not create the collector pool. LIVE/OBSERVE do. `SharedMarketConsumer` exits only in OFF.
  - [SharedMarketSource.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketSource.java:23)
  - [SharedMarketSource.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketSource.java:25)
  - [SharedMarketConsumer.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketConsumer.java:136)

- **Confirmed OBSERVE contradiction:** `OBSERVE` makes `sharedMarketEnabled=false`, causing direct WebSocket startup; its shared consumer still runs because only OFF exits. This violates AGENTS.md’s mutual-exclusion requirement.
  - [SharedMarketSource.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketSource.java:22)
  - [BinanceWebSocketManager.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/client/binance/websocket/BinanceWebSocketManager.java:95)

  - [AGENTS.md](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/AGENTS.md:52)

- **Replay:** fresh Replay loads every `closed=true` candle through `CandleRepository` and chronologically processes every timeline item. The shown query has no provenance/outcome filter, so BACKFILL/reconciled candles are included.
  - [RegressionTestWorker.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:469)
  - [RegressionTestWorker.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:575)

- **V103 versus B105:** V103 truly requires collector table existence when run. But B105 expressly says its trader-only baseline has no V103 effect, meaning it is intended to prevent V103 from being run on a new trader-only history.
  - [V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/db/migration/V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes.java:43)
  - [B105__fix_153_trader_only_baseline.sql](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/db/migration/B105__fix_153_trader_only_baseline.sql:1)

## OBJECTIONS

1. `OBSERVE` runs incompatible architectures together. This is a confirmed source-level violation of the intended design, not merely a documentation issue.

2. Automatic reconnect/gap recovery is disabled by default, despite AGENTS.md requiring automatic recovery without manual repair. The source contains the mechanism, but the default operational configuration does not activate it.

3. The checked-in YAML contains two top-level `local-candle` mappings.
   - [application.yml](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/application.yml:40)
   - [application.yml](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/application.yml:447)

   This is a material configuration defect. I do not confirm a startup failure without executing the configured Spring YAML loader; depending on parser behavior, it may reject duplicates or discard one mapping. Either outcome needs validation.

4. Bootstrap backfill uses duplicate-key update logic which may finalise a pre-existing open candle without issuing a runtime close token.
   - [CandleRepository.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/repository/CandleRepository.java:173)

   Startup ordering likely prevents a startup race, but coin-activation overlap with an active WebSocket stream remains unresolved from this review.

5. `LocalClosedCandleDetector` retains retry/admission state in memory and only searches a bounded lookback. It does persist an expiry outcome when it reaches expiry while running, which corrects the claim that it never writes one. However, after a sufficiently long outage/crash, an unanalysed closed candle can age out before rediscovery and have no detector-generated terminal outcome.
   - [LocalClosedCandleDetector.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/local/LocalClosedCandleDetector.java:47)
   - [LocalClosedCandleDetector.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/local/LocalClosedCandleDetector.java:99)
   - [LocalClosedCandleDetector.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/local/LocalClosedCandleDetector.java:135)

6. The client uses distinct REST clients and bounded timeouts, but no visible shared rate-limit/ban admission, `429`/`418`, or `Retry-After` handling exists in the inspected Binance client. This conflicts with the pending direction’s shared REST-control requirement.
   - [BinanceMarketDataClient.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/client/binance/BinanceMarketDataClient.java:31)
   - [BinanceMarketDataClient.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/client/binance/BinanceMarketDataClient.java:85)
   - [AGENTS.md](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/AGENTS.md:103)

7. Replay’s fresh-signal route includes all closed candles, whereas production deliberately treats bootstrap/reconciliation data as non-live and non-executable. Existing source offers some price-evidence parity machinery, but the reviewed fresh-replay path does not establish origin/outcome parity.

8. Documentation is materially stale:
   - AGENTS.md says local-candle is not implemented/deployed, yet the source implements it as the default OFF path.
   - `codex-docs` and `claude-docs` say automatic reconciliation is pending/no automatic repair, but source contains it.
   - Conversely, the docs’ V103-only bootstrap blocker is overstated because B105 is now present. B105 is not proof of a working fresh install; it is only source evidence of an attempted installer policy.

## UNKNOWNS

- Production mode, deployed artifact, external overrides, migration state, and whether reconciliation is enabled.
- Whether the duplicate YAML mapping is rejected or accepted by the actual resolved Spring Boot dependency set.
- Whether B105 actually works on empty MySQL 8 with the project’s Flyway version.
- MySQL concurrency, crash/restart behavior, Java 21 validation, and Replay parity execution results.
- Whether activation backfill can overlap an active local WebSocket lane.
- Whether any unreviewed replay/parity component compensates for the origin/outcome mismatch.
- Open-position protection during application replacement remains an explicit unresolved release gate.
